# Review pack: avot perek-5-04 (Pirkei Avot 5:19–5:23)

Brief: `briefs/review.md`. Write: `works/avot/stage/review/perek-5-04.json`. Check: `go run ./cmd/tr check avot perek-5-04`.

## Pirkei Avot 5:19

Hebrew (Torat Emet): כָּל מִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּיָדוֹ שְׁלשָׁה דְבָרִים הַלָּלוּ, מִתַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל אַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ. וּשְׁלשָׁה דְבָרִים אֲחֵרִים, מִתַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל בִּלְעָם הָרָשָׁע. עַיִן טוֹבָה, וְרוּחַ נְמוּכָה, וְנֶפֶשׁ שְׁפָלָה, מִתַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל אַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ. עַיִן רָעָה, וְרוּחַ גְּבוֹהָה, וְנֶפֶשׁ רְחָבָה, מִתַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל בִּלְעָם הָרָשָׁע. מַה בֵּין תַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל אַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ לְתַלְמִידָיו שֶׁל בִּלְעָם הָרָשָׁע. תַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל אַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ, אוֹכְלִין בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וְנוֹחֲלִין בָּעוֹלָם הַבָּא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (משלי ח) לְהַנְחִיל אֹהֲבַי יֵשׁ, וְאֹצְרֹתֵיהֶם אֲמַלֵּא. אֲבָל תַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל בִּלְעָם הָרָשָׁע יוֹרְשִׁין גֵּיהִנֹּם וְיוֹרְדִין לִבְאֵר שַׁחַת, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (תהלים נה) וְאַתָּה אֱלֹהִים תּוֹרִידֵם לִבְאֵר שַׁחַת, אַנְשֵׁי דָמִים וּמִרְמָה לֹא יֶחֱצוּ יְמֵיהֶם, וַאֲנִי אֶבְטַח בָּךְ:

- `bartenura:5:19:4` Bartenura **normative** on 5:19.23 5:19.24 «וְנֶפֶשׁ שְׁפָלָה» (matched; filed at 5:19) DH «וְנֶפֶשׁ שְׁפָלָה.»: זְהִירוּת וְהַפְּרִישָׁה מִן הַתַּאֲווֹת. וּמָצִינוּ זֶה בְּאַבְרָהָם, דִּכְתִיב (שם יב) הִנֵּה נָא יָדַעְתִּי כִּי אִשָּׁה יְפַת מַרְאֶה אָתְּ, שֶׁעַד עַכְשָׁיו לֹא הִכִּיר בָּהּ מֵרֹב צְנִיעוּת. וּבְבִלְעָם אַשְׁכְּחַן עַיִן רָעָה, שֶׁהָיָה יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁהָיָה רַע בְּעֵינֵי הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁיֵּלֵךְ אֵצֶל בָּלָק וְהָיָה הוֹלֵךְ כְּדֵי לִטֹּל שָׂכָר, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר כב) אִם יִתֶּן לִי בָלָק מְלֹא בֵיתוֹ כֶּסֶף וְזָהָב:

- `rambam:5:19:1` Rambam **normative** on 5:19.19 5:19.20 «עַיִן טוֹבָה» (matched-unbolded; filed at 5:19) DH «עין טובה»: עין טובה כבר בארנו פעמים רבות שענין עין טובה היא ההסתפקות ונפש שפלה היא הזהירות ורוח נמוכה היא הענוה יתירה כמו שהתבאר בפרק שלפני זה והשלש שכנגדן הוא החריצות לקנות הממון והוא עין רעה ורוב התאוה והיא נפש רחבה והגאוה והיא רוח גבוהה והשלש מעלות ההן נתפרסמו לאברהם אבינו ומפני זה יקרא כל מי שימצאו בו אלו המעלו' מתלמידיו של אברהם אבינו מפני שהתנהג במדותיו. וכל מי שימצאו בו אלו השלשה פחיתיות הוא מתלמידיו של בלעם הרשע אחר שהתנהג במדותיו ואני אזכיר המקומות שהתבארו בהן אלו המעלות לאברהם והפחיתיות ההן כולם לבלעם והן נמצאות כולן בתורה אמנם ההסתפקות באברהם הוא אמרו למלך סדום אם מחוט ועד שרוך נעל ואם אקח מכל אשר לך וגו' וזה תכלית ההסתפקות והוא שיניח אדם ממון גדול ולא יהנה ממנו אפילו בדבר מועט. אבל זהירתו אמרו לשרה ביום בואו מצרימה. הנה נא ידעתי כי אשה יפת מראה את ובא בפי' שלא הסתכל בצורתה הסתכלות שלימה רק ביום ההוא וזה תכלית הזהירות ועוד אמרו על הגר אחר שלקחה הנה שפחתך בידך עשי לה כטוב בעיניך מורה שלא היה לו רצון להתענג עמה וכן כשביקשה ממנו שרה לגרשה עם ישמעאל עד שתמנעהו מנטות אחריה בעבור המשגל העיד הכתוב שהרע בעיניו על ישמעאל לבד והוא אמרו וירע הדבר מאוד בעיני אברהם על אודות בנו: אלו כולם מופתי הזהירות. ואמנם ענותנותו הוא אמרו ואנכי עפר ואפר. והתפרסם לבלעם הרשע החריצות על הממון והוא בואו מארם נהרים בעבור הממון אשר נשכר בו לקלל את ישראל והוא אמרו יתברך אשר שכר עליך את בלעם בן בעור וגו'. ואמנם רוב תאותו בענין המשגל היא סבת עצתו לבלק שיפקיר הנשים לזנות עם ישראל וישים זונות מפורסמות כי לולי רוב התאוה אשר היתה לו והיות הזנות טוב בעיניו לא היה מצוה בו שמצות בני אדם אינם רק לפי דעתם כי הטובים לא יצוו ברע אבל יזהירו ממנו ולשון הכתוב הן הנה היו לבני ישראל בדבר בלעם וגו' וחכמים אמרו בלעם בועל אתונו היה ואין ספק בו שמי שזה דעתו בזה תהיה פעולתו. ואמנם גאותו אמרו נאם שומע אמרי אל ואמנם הביאו ראיה על בלעם מאמרו ואתה אלהים תורידם לבאר שחת אנשי דמים וגו' פירוש מפני שהיה איש הדמים שהוא היה סיבת מות ישראל במגפה והוא ג"כ איש מרמה בעשותו תחבולות לפעולת הרע: והביאו ראיה על תלמידי אברהם מאומרו להנחיל אוהבי יש ואוצרותיהם אמלא כמו שקראו זרע אברהם אוהבי:
- `rabbeinu-yonah:5:19:1` Rabbeinu Yonah on 5:19.1 5:19.2 5:19.3 5:19.5 5:19.6 «כָּל מִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ שְׁלשָׁה דְבָרִים» (matched; filed at 5:19) DH «כל מי שיש בו שלשה דברים וכו'.»: היה אפשר לתנא לקצר ושלא להאריך כלל ופרט והיה יכול לומר כל מי שיש בידו עין טובה ורוח נמוכה ונפש שפלה הוא מתלמידיו של אברהם אבינו וכל מי שיש בידו עין רעה ורוח גבוהה ונפש רחבה מתלמידיו של בלעם הרשע ולמה תנא דרך כלל והדר מפרש דרך פרט. אלא בא ללמדינו שבג' דברים האלה אשר זכר ראשונה כוללים כל השלימות ואם רבו חלקיו וכן השלשה דברים ההפכיים להם כוללים כל הפחיתיות. וזה כי אם היה מקצר על דרך שאמר כי היה נשמע שמי שיהיו לו הג' דברים הנזכרים הוא מתלמידיו של אברהם אבינו אך לתלמידיו של אברהם אבינו יהיו לו שלמיות רבות לאלפים ולרבבות זולת אלה אשר על כן אמר ראשונה שמי שיהיו לו השלשה דברים האלה אשר יזכור והוא מתלמידיו של אברהם אבינו ואח"כ פירש שתלמידיו של אברהם אבינו היה עין טובה וגו' כלומר כל שלמותם על שעליהם היו נקראים תלמידיו היו עין טובה וגו' ועל כן לא אמר תלמידיו של אברהם אבינו היה להם עין טובה וכו' שהיה להם אלה הג' דברים עם שהיו להם עוד דברים אחרים כאלה. אך באומרו תלמידיו של אברהם אבינו עין טובה וכו' רמז שאלה הג' דברים היו הגורמים היותם מתלמידיו של אברהם אבינו וכו'. וכן ההפך לזה בתלמידיו של בלעם. כי לא אמר תלמידיו של בלעם היה להם עין רעה וכו' להורות שכל עצמות הלימוד שלמדו מבלעם הרשע היו הג' דברים הללו בלתי רעות אחרות [רק הג' דברים האלה הם] אשר עליהם היו תלמידי בלעם כי בהם היו כללו' כל הרעו' ואם רבו חלקיהן:

### Translation under review (5:19)

English: Whoever has these three things is of the disciples of Abraham our father; and three other things, of the disciples of Balaam the wicked. A good eye, a humble spirit, and a modest soul: of the disciples of Abraham our father. An evil eye, a haughty spirit, and a broad soul: of the disciples of Balaam the wicked. What is the difference between the disciples of Abraham our father and the disciples of Balaam the wicked? The disciples of Abraham our father eat in this world and inherit in the world to come, as it is said: "To bequeath to those who love me substance, and I will fill their treasuries." But the disciples of Balaam the wicked inherit Gehinnom and go down to the pit of destruction, as it is said: "And You, God, will bring them down to the pit of destruction; men of blood and deceit shall not live out half their days; but I will trust in You."

Literal: all who that-there-is in-his-hand three things these, from-his-disciples of Abraham our-father. and-three things other, from-his-disciples of Balaam the-wicked. eye good, and-spirit lowly, and-soul low, from-his-disciples of Abraham our-father. eye evil, and-spirit high, and-soul broad, from-his-disciples of Balaam the-wicked. what between his-disciples of Abraham our-father to-his-disciples of Balaam the-wicked? his-disciples of Abraham our-father eat in-the-world the-this and-inherit in-the-world the-coming, that-it-was-said (Proverbs 8) to-bequeath those-who-love-me substance, and-their-treasuries I-will-fill. but his-disciples of Balaam the-wicked inherit Gehinnom and-go-down to-pit-of destruction, that-it-was-said (Psalms 55) and-you God you-will-bring-them-down to-pit-of destruction, men-of blood and-deceit not they-will-halve their-days, and-I I-will-trust in-you.

- 5:19.1 5:19.2 | Whoever | 'every who': the indefinite 'whoever'

Grammar notes:
- The mishnah first states the matter in general terms (whoever has these three things) and then names the things; Rabbeinu Yonah draws from this that these three things are the whole of what makes one a disciple of Abraham or of Balaam.
- The two lists are exact opposites, member for member: good eye / evil eye, lowly spirit / high spirit, low soul / broad soul. The two sets are given in parallel clauses with no verb: 'a good eye ... of the disciples of Abraham'.
- מִתַּלְמִידָיו שֶׁל is the Mishnaic genitive with שֶׁל ('of') and a pronominal suffix anticipating the owner: lit. 'from his disciples, of Abraham'. The מִ is partitive.
- The two proof texts are Prov 8:21 and Ps 55:24. In the second the verse is cut after וַאֲנִי אֶבְטַח בָּךְ, which does not describe Balaam's disciples but ends the verse as written.
- Aramaic-style plural participles in -ין (אוֹכְלִין, נוֹחֲלִין, יוֹרְשִׁין, יוֹרְדִין) are normal Mishnaic Hebrew; the mishnah is Hebrew, not Aramaic.

Translator's note: Translation choices. עַיִן טוֹבָה / רָעָה are kept as the literal 'good eye' / 'evil eye', with the commentators' definitions (Bartenura, Rambam: contentment vs. greed) given in the notes. רוּחַ נְמוּכָה 'humble spirit', נֶפֶשׁ שְׁפָלָה 'modest soul' (Bartenura: restraint and abstinence from desires; Rambam: caution), נֶפֶשׁ רְחָבָה 'broad soul' (great craving). אֹהֲבַי is 'those who love me' (Bartenura reads the verse as naming Abraham 'the lover', Isa 41:8). בְּאֵר שַׁחַת is 'the pit of destruction'; שַׁחַת is both 'pit' and 'destruction/corruption'. Gehinnom is left in Hebrew form per the decisions.

Commentary rulings:
- `bartenura:5:19:1` follows: Bartenura: 'of his disciples' means one who learns from him and walks in his ways. The English keeps 'of the disciples' with that sense.
- `bartenura:5:19:2` follows: Bartenura: a good eye is being content with what one has and not coveting others' money; proof Gen 14:23 (Abraham to the king of Sodom: not a thread or a sandal-strap). The English keeps the idiom and the notes give the sense.
- `bartenura:5:19:3` follows: Bartenura: רוּחַ נְמוּכָה is extra humility (עֲנָוָה יְתֵרָה), as Abraham says 'I am dust and ashes' (Gen 18:27).
- `bartenura:5:19:4` follows: Bartenura: נֶפֶשׁ שְׁפָלָה is caution and abstinence from desires; Abraham 'knew' Sarah's beauty only late (Gen 12:11) out of modesty. He adds that Balaam's evil eye shows in his going for payment (Num 22:18).
- `bartenura:5:19:5` follows: Bartenura: Balaam's haughty spirit, shown by 'the utterance of one who hears the words of God and knows the knowledge of the Most High' (Num 24:16).
- `bartenura:5:19:6` follows: Bartenura: נֶפֶשׁ רְחָבָה is great appetite; otherwise Balaam would not have advised exposing the daughters of Moab to immorality; the sages say he lay with his ass (Sanhedrin 105a). The English 'broad soul' is literal and this is the sense.
- `bartenura:5:19:7` follows: Bartenura: Abraham is called 'lover' (Isa 41:8); יֵשׁ is the world to come and 'their treasuries I will fill' is this world. The English takes יֵשׁ as 'substance' with this reading noted; the order of worlds in the verse is the reverse of the mishnah's.
- `bartenura:5:19:8` follows: Bartenura: Balaam is called a 'man of blood' because by his counsel twenty-four thousand of Israel fell. The English 'men of blood' keeps the verse's plural.
- `rambam:5:17:1` not-applicable: Rambam: disputes for the sake of truth endure. His comment on 'this' in 5:17 about reward and punishment; it does not bear on the wording of 5:19.
- `rambam:5:18:1` not-applicable: Rambam: whoever makes others righteous is rewarded by being kept from sin, and whoever leads others astray is punished by being kept from repentance. A comment on the previous mishnah; no bearing on the English here.
- `rambam:5:19:1` follows: Rambam: good eye is contentment, lowly soul is caution, humble spirit is extra humility; their opposites are diligence in acquiring wealth (evil eye), great lust (broad soul) and pride (haughty spirit). He documents each in Abraham and Balaam. The English follows these identifications in its notes.
- apparatus: Prov 8:21: לְהַנְחִיל אֹהֲבַי יֵשׁ וְאֹצְרֹתֵיהֶם אֲמַלֵּא.
- apparatus: Ps 55:24: וְאַתָּה אֱלֹהִים תּוֹרִידֵם לִבְאֵר שַׁחַת אַנְשֵׁי דָמִים וּמִרְמָה לֹא יֶחֱצוּ יְמֵיהֶם וַאֲנִי אֶבְטַח בָּךְ.
- apparatus: Variant 5:19.32: Vilna reads גבוה (masculine) for גְּבוֹהָה; with רוּחַ (feminine) the base is the regular agreement.
- apparatus: Variant 5:19.58: Vilna reads הבאין (plural 'those that come') for הַבָּא; the base reads 'the world to come'.
- apparatus: Variant 5:19.63: Vilna reads אוהבי (full spelling) for אֹהֲבַי. Variant 5:19.65: Vilna reads ואוצרותיהם for וְאֹצְרֹתֵיהֶם.
- apparatus: The pack also lists two position-less variants (Vilna reads כא and כד) with no base text; they appear to be verse markers and bear on no word.

## Pirkei Avot 5:20

Hebrew (Torat Emet): יְהוּדָה בֶן תֵּימָא אוֹמֵר, הֱוֵי עַז כַּנָּמֵר, וְקַל כַּנֶּשֶׁר, וְרָץ כַּצְּבִי, וְגִבּוֹר כָּאֲרִי, לַעֲשׂוֹת רְצוֹן אָבִיךָ שֶׁבַּשָּׁמָיִם. הוּא הָיָה אוֹמֵר, עַז פָּנִים לְגֵיהִנֹּם, וּבֹשֶׁת פָּנִים לְגַן עֵדֶן. יְהִי רָצוֹן מִלְּפָנֶיךָ יְיָ אֱלֹהֵינוּ שֶׁתִּבְנֶה עִירְךָ בִּמְהֵרָה בְיָמֵינוּ וְתֵן חֶלְקֵנוּ בְתוֹרָתֶךָ:

### Commentary anchored to this verse

### Translation under review (5:20)

English: Judah son of Tema says: Be bold as a leopard, light as an eagle, swift as a deer, and strong as a lion, to do the will of your Father in Heaven. He used to say: The bold-faced to Gehinnom, and the shamefaced to the Garden of Eden. May it be Your will, Lord our God, that You rebuild Your city speedily in our days, and give us our portion in Your Torah.

Literal: Judah son-of Tema says: be bold like-a-leopard, and-light like-an-eagle, and-running like-a-deer, and-mighty like-a-lion, to-do the-will-of your-Father who-in-the-heavens. he was saying: bold-of face to-Gehinnom, and-shame-of face to-garden-of Eden. may-it-be will from-before-you YHWH our-God that-you-build your-city in-speed in-our-days, and-give our-portion in-your-Torah.

Grammar notes:
- The four animal similes are parallel, each of the form adjective + כְּ + animal: bold / leopard, light / eagle, swift / deer, strong / lion. A single purpose phrase, 'to do the will of your Father in Heaven', governs all four.
- The second saying is an elliptical antithesis with no verb: 'bold of face (goes) to Gehinnom, shame of face to the Garden of Eden'. The two phrases עַז פָּנִים and בֹּשֶׁת פָּנִים are construct chains, with עַז as the noun 'boldness' in the first.
- יְהִי רָצוֹן מִלְּפָנֶיךָ is the standard petition formula ('may it be Your will') and shifts the form to second person address to God: 'Your city, Your Torah'.
- שֶׁתִּבְנֶה is a purpose/content clause: 'that You build'. In the Mishnaic petition, the verb is in the imperfect after the formula; חֶלְקֵנוּ בְתוֹרָתֶךָ 'our portion in Your Torah' is the second request.
- The words עַז כַּנָּמֵר, וְקַל כַּנֶּשֶׁר, וְרָץ כַּצְּבִי, וְגִבּוֹר כָּאֲרִי pair a trait with its image; the first plays on עַז as both 'fierce' and 'brazen' (cf. עַז פָּנִים below).

Translator's note: עַז פָּנִים is rendered 'bold-faced' and בֹּשֶׁת פָּנִים 'shamefaced' to keep the paired construct chains; the sense is 'the brazen' and 'the bashful'. Rambam and Bartenura read the first saying as boldness in learning and in rebuke for the sake of heaven; the second as the condemned shamelessness. The Tetragrammaton is rendered 'Lord'.

Commentary rulings:
- `bartenura:5:20:1` follows: Bartenura: the leopard is born of a forest boar and a lioness, so being a bastard it is brazen though not truly mighty; so be bold and do not be ashamed to ask your teacher what you do not understand, as 'the shy do not learn'.
- `bartenura:5:20:2` follows: Bartenura: 'light as an eagle' is to hasten after your learning without wearying (Isa 40:31: they shall mount up with wings as eagles; they run and do not grow weary).
- `bartenura:5:20:3` follows: Bartenura: 'swift as a deer' is to pursue the commandments.
- `bartenura:5:20:4` follows: Bartenura: 'strong as a lion' is to overcome your inclination away from transgressions.
- `bartenura:5:20:5` follows: Bartenura: brazenness shows on the face (Prov 21:29: a wicked man hardens his face), hence 'bold of face'.
- `bartenura:5:20:6` follows: Bartenura: 'as You have graced us with this trait' (shame, the mark of Abraham's seed: bashful, merciful, doers of kindness; Yevamot 79a), so may it be Your will to rebuild Your city. The English takes this as the link between the two sayings.
- `rambam:5:20:1` follows: Rambam: though he says 'bold of face to Gehinnom', he commands boldness in rebuking teachers and the like, on condition that one's aim is truth, 'to do the will of your Father'; shame is one of the good traits given to this nation, so he prays for the city to be rebuilt.
- apparatus: Isa 40:31 is cited by Bartenura and Rabbeinu Yonah for the eagle and the deer; not quoted in the mishnah.
- apparatus: Variant 5:20.31: Vilna reads ה for the Tetragrammaton written יְיָ in the base text.
- apparatus: The first saying ('Be bold ...') and the second ('bold of face to Gehinnom') use the same root עַז in different senses; the commentators reconcile them (Rambam: boldness for truth is permitted).

## Pirkei Avot 5:21

Hebrew (Torat Emet): הוּא הָיָה אוֹמֵר, בֶּן חָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים לַמִּקְרָא, בֶּן עֶשֶׂר לַמִּשְׁנָה, בֶּן שְׁלשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה לַמִּצְוֹת, בֶּן חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה לַתַּלְמוּד, בֶּן שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לַחֻפָּה, בֶּן עֶשְׂרִים לִרְדֹּף, בֶּן שְׁלשִׁים לַכֹּחַ, בֶּן אַרְבָּעִים לַבִּינָה, בֶּן חֲמִשִּׁים לָעֵצָה, בֶּן שִׁשִּׁים לַזִּקְנָה, בֶּן שִׁבְעִים לַשֵּׂיבָה, בֶּן שְׁמֹנִים לַגְּבוּרָה, בֶּן תִּשְׁעִים לָשׁוּחַ, בֶּן מֵאָה כְּאִלּוּ מֵת וְעָבַר וּבָטֵל מִן הָעוֹלָם:

### Commentary anchored to this verse

- `bartenura:5:21:1` Bartenura **normative** on 5:21.4 5:21.5 5:21.6 5:21.7 «בֶּן חָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים לַמִּקְרָא» (matched; filed at 5:21) DH «בֶּן חָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים לַמִּקְרָא.»: מֵעָרְלָה יָלְפִינַן, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יט) שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים יִהְיֶה לָכֶם עֲרֵלִים וְגוֹ' וּבַשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית יִהְיֶה כָּל פִּרְיוֹ קֹדֶשׁ הִלּוּלִים, שֶׁאָבִיו מְלַמְּדוֹ צוּרַת הָאוֹתִיּוֹת וְהֶכֵּר הַנְּקֻדּוֹת. וּבַשָּׁנָה הַחֲמִישִׁית תֹּאכְלוּ אֶת פִּרְיוֹ לְהוֹסִיף לָכֶם תְּבוּאָתוֹ, מִכָּאן וְאֵילָךְ סְפֵי לֵיהּ כְּתוֹרָא:
- `bartenura:5:21:2` Bartenura **normative** on 5:21.8 5:21.9 5:21.10 «בֶּן עֶשֶׂר לַמִּשְׁנָה» (matched; filed at 5:21) DH «בֶּן עֶשֶׂר לַמִּשְׁנָה.»: שֶׁלּוֹמֵד מִקְרָא חָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים, וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים מִשְׁנָה, וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים גְּמָרָא. דְּאָמַר מַר (חולין כד.) כָּל תַּלְמִיד שֶׁלֹּא רָאָה סִימָן יָפֶה בְּמִשְׁנָתוֹ חָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים שׁוּב אֵינוֹ רוֹאֶה, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ח) זֹאת אֲשֶׁר לַלְוִיִּם מִבֶּן חָמֵשׁ וְעֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה וָמַעְלָה יָבֹא לִצְבֹא צָבָא, שֶׁבָּא וְלוֹמֵד הִלְכוֹת עֲבוֹדָה חָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים, וּבֶן שְׁלֹשִׁים עוֹבֵד:
- `bartenura:5:21:3` Bartenura **normative** on 5:21.8 5:21.12 5:21.13 5:21.14 «בֶּן שְׁלשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה לַמִּצְוֹת» (matched; filed at 5:21) DH «בֶּן שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה לַמִּצְוֹת.»: דִּכְתִיב (שם ה) אִישׁ אוֹ אִשָּׁה כִּי יַעֲשׂוּ מִכָּל חַטֹּאת הָאָדָם, וְגַבֵּי שְׁכֶם כְּתִיב (בראשית לד) וַיִּקְחוּ שְׁנֵי בְנֵי יַעֲקֹב שִׁמְעוֹן וְלֵוִי אֲחֵי דִינָה אִישׁ חַרְבּוֹ, וְלֵוִי בְּאוֹתוֹ פֶּרֶק בֶּן שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה הָיָה, וְקָרֵי לֵיהּ אִישׁ:
- `bartenura:5:21:4` Bartenura **normative** on 5:21.19 5:21.20 5:21.21 5:21.22 «בֶּן שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לַחֻפָּה» (matched; filed at 5:21) DH «בֶּן שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה לַחֻפָּה.»: תְּשַׁע עֶשְׂרֵה אָדָם כְּתוּבִים בְּפָרָשַׁת בְּרֵאשִׁית מִן וַיֹּאמֶר אֱלֹהִים נַעֲשֶׂה אָדָם עַד וַיִּבֶן ה' אֱלֹהִים אֶת הַצֵּלָע, חַד לְגוּפֵיהּ, פָּשׁוּ לֵיהּ שְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר לִדְרָשָׁא:
- `bartenura:5:21:5` Bartenura **normative** on 5:21.23 5:21.24 5:21.25 «בֶּן עֶשְׂרִים לִרְדֹּף» (matched; filed at 5:21) DH «בֶּן עֶשְׂרִים לִרְדֹּף.»: אַחַר מְזוֹנוֹתָיו. לְאַחַר שֶׁלָּמַד מִקְרָא מִשְׁנָה וּגְמָרָא וְנָשָׂא אִשָּׁה וְהוֹלִיד בָּנִים, צָרִיךְ הוּא לַחֲזֹר וּלְבַקֵּשׁ אַחַר מְזוֹנוֹת. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, בֶּן עֶשְׂרִים לִרְדֹּף אוֹתוֹ מִן הַשָּׁמַיִם וּלְהַעֲנִישׁוֹ עַל מַעֲשָׂיו, שֶׁאֵין בֵּית דִּין שֶׁל מַעֲלָה מַעֲנִישִׁין פָּחוֹת מִבֶּן עֶשְׂרִים:
- `bartenura:5:21:6` Bartenura **normative** on 5:21.23 5:21.27 5:21.28 «בֶּן שְׁלשִׁים לַכֹּחַ» (matched; filed at 5:21) DH «בֶּן שְׁלֹשִׁים לַכֹּחַ.»: שֶׁהַלְוִיִּם הָיוּ מְקִימִים אֶת הַמִּשְׁכָּן וּמְפָרְקִין וְטוֹעֲנִין אֶת הָעֲגָלוֹת וְנוֹשְׂאִין בַּכָּתֵף מִבֶּן שְׁלֹשִׁים שָׁנָה וָמַעְלָה:
- `bartenura:5:21:7` Bartenura **normative** on 5:21.26 5:21.30 5:21.31 «בֶּן אַרְבָּעִים לַבִּינָה» (matched; filed at 5:21) DH «בֶּן אַרְבָּעִים לַבִּינָה.»: שֶׁלְּאַחַר אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה שֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּמִּדְבָּר אָמַר לָהֶם מֹשֶׁה (דברים כט) וְלֹא נָתַן ה' לָכֶם לֵב לָדַעַת וְעֵינַיִם לִרְאוֹת וְאָזְנַיִם לִשְׁמֹעַ עַד הַיּוֹם הַזֶּה:
- `bartenura:5:21:8` Bartenura **normative** on 5:21.29 5:21.33 5:21.34 «בֶּן חֲמִשִּׁים לָעֵצָה» (matched; filed at 5:21) DH «בֶּן חֲמִשִּׁים לָעֵצָה.»: שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בַּלְּוִיִּם (במדבר ח) וּמִבֶּן חֲמִשִּׁים שָׁנָה יָשׁוּב מִצְּבָא הָעֲבוֹדָה וְלֹא יַעֲבֹד עוֹד וְשֵׁרֵת אֶת אֶחָיו וְגוֹ', וּמַהוּ הַשֵּׁרוּת, שֶׁיִּתֵּן לָהֶם עֵצָה:
- `bartenura:5:21:9` Bartenura **normative** on 5:21.32 5:21.36 5:21.37 «בֶּן שִׁשִּׁים לַזִּקְנָה» (matched; filed at 5:21) DH «בֶּן שִׁשִּׁים לַזִּקְנָה.»: דִּכְתִיב (איוב ה) תָּבֹא בְכֶלַח אֱלֵי קָבֶר. בכל"ח בַּגִּימַטְרִיָּא שִׁשִּׁים:

### Translation under review (5:21)

English: He used to say: At five years old, for Scripture; at ten, for Mishnah; at thirteen, for the commandments; at fifteen, for Talmud; at eighteen, for the wedding canopy; at twenty, for pursuit; at thirty, for strength; at forty, for understanding; at fifty, for counsel; at sixty, for old age; at seventy, for gray hair; at eighty, for might; at ninety, for stooping; at a hundred, it is as if he had died and passed away and ceased from the world.

Literal: he was saying: son-of five years to-Scripture, son-of ten to-Mishnah, son-of thirteen to-the-commandments, son-of fifteen to-Talmud, son-of eighteen to-the-canopy, son-of twenty to-pursue, son-of thirty to-strength, son-of forty to-understanding, son-of fifty to-counsel, son-of sixty to-old-age, son-of seventy to-grey-hair, son-of eighty to-might, son-of ninety to-stoop, son-of hundred as-if dead and-passed and-ceased from-the-world.

Grammar notes:
- Each stage is an elliptical clause of the form 'son of N (years) to X': the verb 'is fit' is understood. The ל prefix is 'for' (fit for, ready for).
- The ages count by tens from twenty to a hundred; בֶּן שָׁנִים is a fixed idiom for age. Gender of the numerals follows the noun they do not name: שָׁנָה (feminine) takes חָמֵשׁ, עֶשֶׂר, שְׁלשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה, etc.
- Each ל-noun is a stage of life or a pursuit: מִקְרָא (Bible), מִשְׁנָה (oral teaching), מִצְוֹת (commandments), תַּלְמוּד (study), חֻפָּה (marriage), רדף (pursuit), כֹּחַ (strength), בִּינָה, עֵצָה, זִקְנָה, שֵׂיבָה, גְּבוּרָה, שׁוּחַ.
- The last clause is a full sentence: כְּאִלּוּ מֵת וְעָבַר וּבָטֵל מִן הָעוֹלָם, with a participle/adjective and two perfects: 'as if he had died and passed and ceased from the world'.

Translator's note: Translated with the plain sense of each stage. Bartenura derives the first stages from the law of orlah (Lev 19:23-25) and the Levites (Num 8). For לִרְדֹּף I keep 'pursuit' and record both of his readings (livelihood; or being pursued by the heavenly court) in the notes. לָשׁוּחַ is 'stooping' (Bartenura: walking bent and bowed).

Commentary rulings:
- `bartenura:5:21:1` follows: Bartenura: five years for Scripture is learned from orlah (Lev 19:23-25): the fourth year is holy, the father teaching the shape of the letters and the vowel points; from the fifth, he is to be strengthened like an ox.
- `bartenura:5:21:2` follows: Bartenura: five years Scripture, five Mishnah, five Gemara; a student who has seen no sign of success in five years will not (Hullin 24a); the Levites learned the laws of service five years (Num 8:24).
- `bartenura:5:21:3` follows: Bartenura: thirteen for the commandments: Num 5:6 'a man or woman'; Levi at thirteen is called 'man' (Gen 34:25).
- `bartenura:5:21:4` follows: Bartenura: nineteen occurrences of 'adam' in the first section of Genesis; one for the body itself, eighteen remain for the derashah (the wedding canopy).
- `bartenura:5:21:5` records: Bartenura: at twenty, to pursue a livelihood, after learning, marrying and having children. Another reading: to be pursued from Heaven, since the heavenly court does not punish before twenty. The English keeps the neutral 'pursuit'.
- `bartenura:5:21:6` follows: Bartenura: thirty for strength: the Levites set up and dismantled the tabernacle and carried it from the age of thirty.
- `bartenura:5:21:7` follows: Bartenura: forty for understanding: after forty years in the wilderness Moses said 'the Lord has not given you a heart to know until this day' (Deut 29:3).
- `bartenura:5:21:8` follows: Bartenura: fifty for counsel: the Levites retired from service at fifty and 'served' their brothers, which is giving counsel (Num 8:25).
- `bartenura:5:21:9` follows: Bartenura: sixty for old age: Job 5:26, 'you shall come to the grave in ripe age' (בכלח), whose numerical value is sixty.
- `bartenura:5:21:10` follows: Bartenura: seventy for gray old age: David died 'in good old age' (1 Chr 29:28) and lived seventy years.
- `bartenura:5:21:11` follows: Bartenura: eighty for might: Ps 90:10, 'and if by reason of strength eighty years'.
- `bartenura:5:21:12` follows: Bartenura: ninety to stoop: one walks bent and bowed. Others: from the word for a deep pit (שׁוּחָה).
- `rambam:5:23:1` not-applicable: Rambam (anchored from 5:23): reward is according to toil; knowledge learned with labor and awe lasts. It concerns Ben He He's saying in 5:23, not this mishnah.
- apparatus: Bartenura's proof texts: Lev 19:23-25; Num 8:24-25; Gen 34:25; Deut 29:3; Job 5:26; 1 Chr 29:28; Ps 90:10.
- apparatus: Variants (spelling only): 5:21.20 שמונה, 5:21.22 לחופה, 5:21.25 לרדוף, 5:21.42 שמונים, 5:21.49 כאילו.
- apparatus: Rabbeinu Yonah on לָשׁוּחַ: from שיח 'speech' (one speaks only words of Torah) or 'pit/cave' (fit for burial).

## Pirkei Avot 5:22

Hebrew (Torat Emet): בֶּן בַּג בַּג אוֹמֵר, הֲפֹךְ בָּהּ וַהֲפֹךְ בָּהּ, דְּכֹלָּא בָהּ. וּבָהּ תֶּחֱזֵי, וְסִיב וּבְלֵה בָהּ, וּמִנַּהּ לֹא תָזוּעַ, שֶׁאֵין לְךָ מִדָּה טוֹבָה הֵימֶנָּה:

### Commentary anchored to this verse

- `bartenura:5:22:1` Bartenura **normative** on 5:22.5 5:22.6 5:22.7 5:22.8 «הֲפֹךְ בָּהּ וַהֲפֹךְ בָּהּ» (matched; filed at 5:22) DH «הֲפֹךְ בָּהּ וַהֲפֹךְ בָּהּ.»: בַּתּוֹרָה:

### Translation under review (5:22)

English: Ben Bag Bag says: Turn it and turn it, for everything is in it; and in it you will see, and grow old and worn in it, and do not move from it, for you have no quality better than it.

Literal: son-of Bag Bag says: turn in-it and-turn in-it, that-all in-it; and-in-it you-will-see, and-grow-old and-wear-out in-it, and-from-it not you-move, that-not-is to-you quality good from-it.

- 5:22.1 5:22.2 5:22.3 | Ben Bag Bag | 'son-of Bag Bag': a fixed name
- 5:22.4 | says | 'says'
- 5:22.5 5:22.6 | Turn it | 'turn in-it'
- 5:22.7 5:22.8 | and turn it | 'and-turn in-it'
- 5:22.9 5:22.10 | for everything is in it | 'that-all in-it' (Aramaic)
- 5:22.11 5:22.12 | and in it you will see | 'and-in-it you-will-see' (Aramaic)
- 5:22.13 5:22.14 5:22.15 | and grow old and worn in it | 'and-grow-old and-wear-out in-it' (Aramaic)
- 5:22.16 5:22.17 5:22.18 | and do not move from it | 'and-from-it not you-move' (Aramaic)
- 5:22.19 5:22.20 | for you have no | 'that-not-is to-you'
- 5:22.21 5:22.22 5:22.23 | quality better than it | 'quality good from-it'

Grammar notes:
- The saying mixes Hebrew and Aramaic: הֲפֹךְ בָּהּ וַהֲפֹךְ בָּהּ is Hebrew; דְּכֹלָּא בָהּ וּבָהּ תֶּחֱזֵי וְסִיב וּבְלֵה בָהּ וּמִנַּהּ לָא תָזוּעַ is Aramaic; שֶׁאֵין לְךָ מִדָּה טוֹבָה הֵימֶנָּה is Hebrew again.
- The feminine בָּהּ refers back to the Torah, as the commentators say (the mishnah does not name it: it follows the previous saying).
- The imperatives הֲפֹךְ, סִיב, בְּלֵה are paired: turn it over and over; grow old and wear out in it.
- The negation לֹא תָזוּעַ is a prohibition in Aramaic style ('do not move'); the final clause gives the reason with שֶׁאֵין לְךָ 'for you have not'.
- מִדָּה 'measure, quality'; the English 'quality' also allows 'virtue' or 'standard'.

Translator's note: The pronoun 'it' is the Torah (Bartenura, Rambam). The Aramaic words are translated without comment in the English; the notes and grammar record where the Aramaic begins and ends.

Commentary rulings:
- `bartenura:5:22:1` follows: Bartenura: 'turn it and turn it': in the Torah. The English 'it' is the Torah.
- `bartenura:5:22:2` follows: Bartenura: 'for everything is in it': that you will find everything in it.
- `bartenura:5:22:3` follows: Bartenura: even to old age and gray hair do not abandon it.
- `bartenura:5:22:4` follows: Bartenura: do not say you have learned the wisdom of Israel and will go learn Greek wisdom; it may be learned only at a time when thought of Torah is forbidden (bathhouse), or in the hour that is neither day nor night (R. Joshua), since 'you shall meditate on it day and night'. The English keeps 'do not move from it'.
- `rambam:5:22:1` follows: Rambam: turn it and meditate in it because everything is in it; 'and in it you will see' means you will see the truth with the eye of the intellect (as the Targum renders 'and he saw' with 'and he chazah'); then grow old in it and do not turn from it to another.
- apparatus: Rabbeinu Yonah (anchored 5:22.5-6): review words of Torah, for all the wisdom of the world is included in it.
- apparatus: Variants: 5:22.5 הפוך, 5:22.7 והפוך (Vilna), 5:22.9 דכולה, 5:22.16 ומינה (Vilna spellings).
- apparatus: Aramaic is in the Hebrew of the base text; בָהּ/בָּהּ vary in dagesh only.

## Pirkei Avot 5:23

### Commentary anchored to this verse

- `bartenura:5:23:1` Bartenura **normative** on 5:23.5 5:23.6 5:23.7 «לְפוּם צַעֲרָא אַגְרָא» (matched; filed at 5:23) DH «לְפוּם צַעֲרָא אַגְרָא.»: כְּפִי רֹב הַצַּעַר שֶׁאַתָּה סוֹבֵל בְּלִמּוּד הַתּוֹרָה וַעֲשִׂיַּת הַמִּצְוָה, יִהְיֶה שְׂכָרְךָ מְרֻבֶּה:

### Translation under review (5:23)

English: Ben He He says: According to the toil is the reward.

Literal: son-of He He says: to-mouth-of the-toil the-reward.

| token | gloss | lex | sense | note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 5:23.1 | son |  | 1 | Ben He He: one name (decisions). |
| 5:23.2 | He |  | 1 |  |
| 5:23.3 | He |  | 1 |  |
| 5:23.4 | says |  | 1 |  |
| 5:23.5 | according to |  |  | לְפוּם: Aramaic: 'according to', lit. 'to the mouth of' (פּוּם 'mouth'); an idiom meaning 'in proportion to'. |
| 5:23.6 | the toil |  |  | צַעֲרָא: Aramaic 'the pain/toil/suffering' (emphatic state). |
| 5:23.7 | the reward |  | 1 | אַגְרָא: Aramaic 'the reward' (emphatic state). |

Phrases:
- 5:23.1 5:23.2 5:23.3 | Ben He He | 'son-of He He': a fixed name
- 5:23.4 | says | 'says'
- 5:23.5 5:23.6 | According to the toil | 'to-mouth-of the-toil': 'in proportion to'
- 5:23.7 | is the reward | 'the-reward'

Grammar notes:
- The saying is in Aramaic and has no verb: 'according to the toil, the reward'.
- לְפוּם is lit. 'to the mouth of' and is an idiom for 'in proportion to'; it takes the following noun in the emphatic state.
- The nouns צַעֲרָא and אַגְרָא are Aramaic emphatic-state forms, with the final -א.

Translator's note: I translate צַעֲרָא as 'toil' (Bartenura: the effort one bears in studying Torah and doing the commandment), not 'suffering', and supply 'is' in the English.

Commentary rulings:
- `bartenura:5:23:1` follows: Bartenura: according to the great effort you bear in studying the Torah and doing the commandment, your reward will be greater. The English 'toil' follows this.
- apparatus: The Aramaic saying is the last in the chapter before the mishnah ending this unit; no variants are recorded.

### `jastrow:אֱלוֹהַּ` אֱלוֹהַּ (m.): v. אֵל) God. Pl. majest. אֱלֹהִים, cmp. אֵל.—האלהים by God! Sabb. 145ᵃ; a. fr. [ד is freq. substituted for ה as אלדים, אלדי, ידוד esp. in Targ. Y. ed. Sab.] אלקים, ch. אלקא, adopted in order to avoid uttering the divine name. B. Kam. 106ᵃ; cmp. אלעיקי, איפופי …
- `1` v. אֵל) God. Pl. majest. אֱלֹהִים, cmp. אֵל.—האלהים by God! Sabb. 145ᵃ; a. fr. [ד is freq. substituted for ה as אלדים, אלדי, ידוד esp. in Targ. Y. ed. Sab.] אלקים, ch. אלקא, adopted in order to avoid uttering the divine name. B. Kam. 106ᵃ; cmp. אלעיקי, איפופי …
- `2` deities, powers. Y. Ber. IX, beg. 12ᵈ. Lev. R. s. 4.
- `3` biblical verses containing the word El, Elohim &c. Y. R. Hash. IV, 59ᶜ.

### `jastrow:אֲגַר I` אֲגַר I: ; Pa. אַגַּר to heap up. Targ. Y. Deut. I, 16 דמְאַגַּר וכ׳ (ed. Vien. מֶאֱגַר—) who heaps up litigations, v. foreg. [Targ. Ps. CIV, 7 למיגר Ms., v. נְגַד.]
- `1` ; Pa. אַגַּר to heap up. Targ. Y. Deut. I, 16 דמְאַגַּר וכ׳ (ed. Vien. מֶאֱגַר—) who heaps up litigations, v. foreg. [Targ. Ps. CIV, 7 למיגר Ms., v. נְגַד.]

### `jastrow:אֲגַר II` אֲגַר II: (אָגַר) (√אג, akin to חכר; v. foreg.) prop. to tie, whence to hire, employ, rent. Targ. Gen. XXX, 16; a. fr.—Koh. R. to IV, 6; Lev. R. s. 3 beg. (prov.) גינא וכ׳ דא׳ he who rents one garden will eat birds; him who rents gardens, the birds will eat. Git. 73ᵃ אג…
- `1` (אָגַר) (√אג, akin to חכר; v. foreg.) prop. to tie, whence to hire, employ, rent. Targ. Gen. XXX, 16; a. fr.—Koh. R. to IV, 6; Lev. R. s. 3 beg. (prov.) גינא וכ׳ דא׳ he who rents one garden will eat birds; him who rents gardens, the birds will eat. Git. 73ᵃ אג…
- `2.1` Af. same. Targ. Y. Deut. XXIII, 5 (4).—B. Mets. 77ᵃ (interch. with Pe.) (אגירי) אוגיר אגורי engages laborers.
- `2.2` Af. to rent out, lease. Erub. 63ᵇ אוֹגַר לן רשותך lease to us thy property. Y. Dem. VI, 25ᵇ top. Y. Taan. I, 64ᵇ bot. אוגרית חמרי I hired my ass out.
- `4.1` Ithpe. (contr. of איתאגר) to be hired, to work as a laborer. Targ. O. Deut. XXIII, 25; a. e.—Yoma 20ᵇ (prov.) אי תגרת וכ׳ (combine into one w.) when thou hast hired thyself out to one, comb his wool (shrink from no labor).

### `jastrow:אֲגַר III` אֲגַר III (m.): (foreg.) rent, wages; reward, profit. Targ. Gen. XV, 1; a. fr.—B. Mets. 63ᵇ א׳ נטר ליה compensation for waiting (giving time for delivery), i.e. advancing the money to the seller. Ib. 68ᵇ פלגא בא׳ כ׳ half profit or loss. Ib. 69ᵇ א׳ ופגרא payment for carrying f…
- `1` (foreg.) rent, wages; reward, profit. Targ. Gen. XV, 1; a. fr.—B. Mets. 63ᵇ א׳ נטר ליה compensation for waiting (giving time for delivery), i.e. advancing the money to the seller. Ib. 68ᵇ פלגא בא׳ כ׳ half profit or loss. Ib. 69ᵇ א׳ ופגרא payment for carrying f…

### `jastrow:אֲכַל` אֲכַל: same.—Inf. מֵיכַל, מֵיכוּל. Targ. Gen. II, 16; a. fr.—Y. Ter. VIII, 46ᵃ כד אתון יֵיכלון when they came and were about eating; ib. (more corr.) אתון מיכל; a. fr.
- `2` same.—Inf. מֵיכַל, מֵיכוּל. Targ. Gen. II, 16; a. fr.—Y. Ter. VIII, 46ᵃ כד אתון יֵיכלון when they came and were about eating; ib. (more corr.) אתון מיכל; a. fr.
- `3` with קרצי prop. to eat (the bread of) destruction, eat the informer’s bread, hence to inform against. Dan. III, 8; VI, 25.—Targ. Ps. XV, 3; a. fr.—Gitt. 56ᵃ איכול בהו קו׳; Lam. R. to IV, 2 איכול קרצהון I will inform against them.
- `4.1` Af. to give to eat, to support. Y. Ter. X, 47ᵇ bot. אוכלה ליה he gave it to him to eat. Gen. R. s. 48, end (read:) אוכלית אשקית לוי hast thou given (thy guest) to eat? to drink? do escort him, i.e. perform thy duties fully. Y. Kidd. I, 61ᵇ הוה מייכל לאבוי וכ׳ fed …
- `5.1` Ithpe. , אִתְאָכִיל to be eaten, consumed. Targ. Ps. LXVIII, 23; a. fr. Tanḥ. Emor, 6 ומה דהבא מִתְאֲכִיל וכ׳; Lev. R. s. 27 ומה זה וכ׳ what is this? do they eat gold &c.? Kidd. 59ᵃ קא מתאכלי were eaten up (spent). [אכלי Af. of כלי to cry, v. כלי.]

- `1` 1) as preced. 1). Targ. Gen. I, 3; a. v. fr.—א׳ מר above you quoted an authority, or, it has been stated. Ber. 2ᵃ; a. fr.—א׳ קרא the Scripture says. Ib. 13ᵃ; a. fr.—Y. Orl. III, end, 63ᵇ, a. fr. אמרונה בשם … (in Bab. heb. אֲמָרוּהָ) they said it in behalf of ……
- `2.1` Ithpe. , contr. אִתְּמַר, אִיתְּ׳ it is said, taught. Targ. Job XXXIV, 31.—Y. Yoma V, end, 43ᵃ אתאמרת, contr. אִתַּמְרַת it has been said with refer. to the opinion of …—אִתְּ׳, אִיתְּ׳ it has been said (above);we have been taught. Succ. 15ᵃ; a. fr.
- `3.1` Ithpa. same. Targ. Gen. XXII, 14; a. e.

### `jastrow:אֲרִי` אֲרִי: Targ. Num. XXIV, 9; a. fr.—B. Mets. 101ᵇ, a. e., v. אֲרב. Ned. 62ᵇ לאברוחי א׳ to drive the lion off, i.e. to get rid of the tax-collector.—Shebu. 22ᵇ, a. fr. א׳ הוא דרביע עלה a lion lies on it, i.e. it is unavailable because it is forbidden.—Ab. Zar. 31ᵇ, a. e…

### `jastrow:אֲרִי I` אֲרִי I: (√אר, cmp. ירי) prop. to point, throw, hence (in a logical sense) to argue, question, discuss, treat. B. Kam. 30ᵇ מדקא ארינן גלל Ar. (ed. איירינן, v. infra) since we pointed out the law about the dung placed in the street as an argument against Rab. Keth. 16ᵃ,…
- `1` (√אר, cmp. ירי) prop. to point, throw, hence (in a logical sense) to argue, question, discuss, treat. B. Kam. 30ᵇ מדקא ארינן גלל Ar. (ed. איירינן, v. infra) since we pointed out the law about the dung placed in the street as an argument against Rab. Keth. 16ᵃ,…
- `2.1` Af. same. Ned. 2ᵇ איירי בהון he does treat thereof.—Pes. 6ᵇ, a. fr. איידי דאיירי ב־ incidentally of treating the question of … Kerith. 14ᵃ, a. fr. (באבר אחד) קא מַיְירֵי the argument is up (about one limb). B. Kam. 30ᵇ כי איירינן when we raised the objection (taki…

### `jastrow:אֲרִי II` אֲרִי II (m.): , v. preced.) [the light-colored]
- `1` , v. preced.) [the light-colored]
- `2` lion. B. Kam. 16ᵇ א׳ תרבות a tamed lion; a. fr.—Transf. brave man; distinguished scholar (opp. שועל). Yeb. 122ᵇ. Kidd. 48ᵇ; Snh. 8ᵇ. Gitt. 83ᵇ; Y. ib. IX, 50ᵃ אין משיבין את הא׳ וכ׳ you must not argue against a lion (scholar) after he is dead. B. Kam. 117ᵃ; a. …
- `3` Leo, Lion the fifth sign of the Zodiac (corresp. to the month of Ab). Yalk. Ex. 418. Pesik. Dibré p. 116ᵃ; Pesik. R. s. 27 (28, p. 133ᵇ, ed. Fr.); v. אֲרִיאֵל.
- `4` homiletic surname of the Lord, Israel &c. Pesik. l. c.; Pesik. R. l. c.; Yalk. Jer. 259, v. אֲרִיאֵל.

### `jastrow:אִין I` אִין I: (Syr. = הֵין, הֶן) yes, indeed. Keth. 65ᵃ השתא א׳ וכ׳ now,—yes, but not before. B. Mets. 98ᵃ חדא א׳ בעידן וכ׳ as regards the one (cow)—well, she died &c.; a. fr.—אֵינִי = אִין הִי is it really so? indeed?, i.e. it cannot be. Ḥull. 96ᵇ א׳ והאמר it cannot be so;…
- `1` (Syr. = הֵין, הֶן) yes, indeed. Keth. 65ᵃ השתא א׳ וכ׳ now,—yes, but not before. B. Mets. 98ᵃ חדא א׳ בעידן וכ׳ as regards the one (cow)—well, she died &c.; a. fr.—אֵינִי = אִין הִי is it really so? indeed?, i.e. it cannot be. Ḥull. 96ᵇ א׳ והאמר it cannot be so;…

### `jastrow:אִישׁ` אִישׁ (m.): , v. אֱינָשׁ) being, man; husband; lord.—א׳ ירושלים a native or citizen of Jerusalem Aboth I, 4; a. fr.—Yoma I, 7 אִישִׁי כה"ג my lord the High-priest. Sot. 17ᵃ א׳ ואשה husband and wife; a. fr.—Pl. אֲנָשִׁים, v. אֱנוֹשׁ.
- `1` , v. אֱינָשׁ) being, man; husband; lord.—א׳ ירושלים a native or citizen of Jerusalem Aboth I, 4; a. fr.—Yoma I, 7 אִישִׁי כה"ג my lord the High-priest. Sot. 17ᵃ א׳ ואשה husband and wife; a. fr.—Pl. אֲנָשִׁים, v. אֱנוֹשׁ.

### `jastrow:אֵין` אֵין: nothing, not. Ber. 5ᵃ אין עוף אלא וכ׳ uf (flight, Job V, 7) means nothing else but &c.; a. v. fr.—אֵינִי, אֵינְךָ not I &c. אֵינִי יודע I do not know; do I not know? B. Mets. 97ᵇ; a. fr. Ber. l. c. but the Holy One … אֵינוֹ כן is not so; a. fr.—אֵינוֹ דין is i…
- `1` nothing, not. Ber. 5ᵃ אין עוף אלא וכ׳ uf (flight, Job V, 7) means nothing else but &c.; a. v. fr.—אֵינִי, אֵינְךָ not I &c. אֵינִי יודע I do not know; do I not know? B. Mets. 97ᵇ; a. fr. Ber. l. c. but the Holy One … אֵינוֹ כן is not so; a. fr.—אֵינוֹ דין is i…

### `jastrow:אֵלּוּ` אֵלּוּ: c. pl. (b. h. , q. v.)
- `1` c. pl. (b. h. , q. v.)
- `2` these, the following. B. Mets. II, 1; a. v. fr.—א׳ וא׳ both. Zeb. V, 2; a. v. fr. *[
- `3` (interrog.) which? Pesik. R. s. 29 (—30, ed. Friedm. p. 138ᵇ) לאילו נאמין in which of them shall we trust? Pesik. Naḥămu p. 127ᵇ sq.; Yalk. Is. 307 לאי זה, לאיזו.]

### `jastrow:אַחַר` אַחַר: v. foreg.), pl. constr. אַחֲרֵי after, behind. Gen. R. s. 44 wherever the Bible uses the preposition aḥaré סמוך, it means in connection with, אחר מופלג while aḥar means without connection (later on). Yoma 6ᵃ אחר א׳ after ‘after’, i.e. some time after the act, …
- `1` v. foreg.), pl. constr. אַחֲרֵי after, behind. Gen. R. s. 44 wherever the Bible uses the preposition aḥaré סמוך, it means in connection with, אחר מופלג while aḥar means without connection (later on). Yoma 6ᵃ אחר א׳ after ‘after’, i.e. some time after the act, …
- `2` (conj. followed by ש־ or ד־) after, since, whereas, because. Ḥull. 29ᵃ מא׳ ששנינו after it has once been stated in the Mishnah … why was it necessary (?) &c. Ib.ᵇ ומאחר דאפי׳ וכ׳ and since it is not even rabbinically unfit, why &c.; a. fr.
- `3` a legal term, meaḥar, the presumption of the truth of one’s statement, because he might have pleaded more profitably, if he had been inclined to lie. Y. Shebu. VI, 36ᵈ bot. אין אומרים בממון מא׳ מאחר וכ׳ in money matters we do not apply the principle of meaḥer,…

### `jastrow:אַתָּה` אַתָּה (m.): , , v. אָנֹכִי) thou. Ber. 11ᵃ; a. fr.—אתה אומר, v. אַתְּ.—Pl. אַתֶּם, אַתֶּן ye. Ber. I, 1; a. fr.—R. Hash. 25ᵃ א׳ א׳ א׳ ג׳ פעמים וכ׳ three times attem is written (אֹתָם, without ו); v. אֲפִילּוּ. Sifra Emor IX, 9; a. fr.
- `1` , , v. אָנֹכִי) thou. Ber. 11ᵃ; a. fr.—אתה אומר, v. אַתְּ.—Pl. אַתֶּם, אַתֶּן ye. Ber. I, 1; a. fr.—R. Hash. 25ᵃ א׳ א׳ א׳ ג׳ פעמים וכ׳ three times attem is written (אֹתָם, without ו); v. אֲפִילּוּ. Sifra Emor IX, 9; a. fr.

### `jastrow:אָב I` אָב I: (Assyr. A-bu, Schr. K. A. T., p. 247) Ab, the fifth month of the Jewish calendar (of thirty days) beginning between the eighth of July and the seventh of August, and ending between the sixth of August and the fifth of September. R. Hash. I, 3, על אב וכ׳ for an…
- `1` (Assyr. A-bu, Schr. K. A. T., p. 247) Ab, the fifth month of the Jewish calendar (of thirty days) beginning between the eighth of July and the seventh of August, and ending between the sixth of August and the fifth of September. R. Hash. I, 3, על אב וכ׳ for an…

### `jastrow:אָב II` אָב II (m.): , const. אֲבִי, אַב [embracer], father, ancestor, progenitor; teacher; chief, leader; author, originator. Ex. R. s. 46 end המגדל אב the educator is the real father. Lev. R. s. 1 אבי החכמה וכ׳, the father of all wisdom, … the father of prophets. Y. Ned. V, 39ᵇ;…
- `1` , const. אֲבִי, אַב [embracer], father, ancestor, progenitor; teacher; chief, leader; author, originator. Ex. R. s. 46 end המגדל אב the educator is the real father. Lev. R. s. 1 אבי החכמה וכ׳, the father of all wisdom, … the father of prophets. Y. Ned. V, 39ᵇ;…
- `2` fathers, ancestors, patriarchs &c. Ber. 26ᵇ prayers א׳ תקנום have been instituted by the Patriarchs; a. fr.—אבות בתי דינין, v. supra. Ḥag. II, 2.—בת א׳ a woman of noble descent. Num. R. s. 1; a. e.—Metaph. principal, chief א׳ מלאכות, v. supra. א׳ הטומא׳ Kel. I…
- `3` Aboth, the first section of the Prayer of Benedictions (v. תְּפִלָּה), so called because it alludes to the Patriarchs. R. Hash. IV, 5; a. fr.
- `4` Aboth, name of a treatise of the Mishnah, containing sayings of Talmudic authorities and belonging to the fourth section, נזיקין, of the Mishnah collection (משניות); also styled פִּרְקֵי א׳ a. מְסֶכֶת א׳. A similar collection of a later date is contained in Ta…

### `jastrow:אָב II ²` אָב II ²: ch., v. אַבָּא
- `1` ch., v. אַבָּא

### `jastrow:אָהַב` אָהַב: to love. Y. Ab. Zar. II beg. 40ᶜ אוֹהֲבָהּ her lover; a. fr. אוהב את הבריות philanthropist. Aboth I, 12; a. fr.—Part. pass. אָהוּב, f. אֲהוּבָה. Yeb. 23ᵃ א׳ בנשואיה beloved (worth loving) for her well chosen marriage. [Y. Ab. Zar. l. c. איהבתו read אוֹהַבְתֹּו…
- `1` to love. Y. Ab. Zar. II beg. 40ᶜ אוֹהֲבָהּ her lover; a. fr. אוהב את הבריות philanthropist. Aboth I, 12; a. fr.—Part. pass. אָהוּב, f. אֲהוּבָה. Yeb. 23ᵃ א׳ בנשואיה beloved (worth loving) for her well chosen marriage. [Y. Ab. Zar. l. c. איהבתו read אוֹהַבְתֹּו…
- `2.1` Nif. , Hithpa. הִתְאָהֵב to be beloved, popular. Lev. R. s. 32, beg., these blows (of persecution) had the effect להֵאָהֵב וכ׳ to make me beloved of my Father in heaven. Yoma 86ᵃ שיהא ש"ש מִתְאָהֵב וכ׳ that the Divine Name may be beloved through thee (that thy doin…
- `4.1` Hif. to make beloved, popular. Tan. d’be El. I, 28 תהא מְאָהֵב ש"ש וכ׳ make the Divine Name &c. (v. supra).—Part. Pu. מְאוּהָב popular. Yalk. Deut. 837.—Cant. R. to I, 1 להַאֲהִיבָן (לאָהֲבָן) לקרבן וכ׳ to make them beloved (of God), draw them nigh (to God) &c. Ib.…

### `jastrow:אָכַל` אָכַל: √אך to rub,
- `1` √אך to rub,
- `2` to gnaw, eat, consume. Inf. in Y. freq. לוֹכַל = לֶאֱכוֹל. Ber. I, 1; a. v. fr.
- `3` trnsf. to absorb, occupy, take away. Y. Shebu. VII, 38ᵃ בשרבית אוֹכֶלֶת בו when interests gnaw on (absorb) the property. Y. Erub. IV, 21ᵈ bot. ד׳ אמות אוֹכְלוֹת וכ׳ four cubits entering into the area of Tiberias. Num. R. s. 4 כמה לוחות אוֹכְלוֹת how much space…
- `4` (euphem.) to sleep with. Keth. V, 9, differ. of opin. ib. 65ᵇ; Y. ib. 30ᵇ top a. bot.
- `5.1` Nif. to be eaten &c. Zeb. I, 3; Ber. I, 1; a. fr.
- `6.1` Pi. to consume, burn. Tam. I, 4 המְאוּכָּלוֹת הפנימיות (Talm. ed. המעו׳) the thoroughly lighted coals in the centre; Y. Yoma II, 39ᶜ
- `7.1` Hif. to give to eat, cause to eat. Keth. V, 3 היבם אינו מַאֵכִיל וכ׳ the Yabam does not transfer the privilege of eating T’rumah to his sister-in-law. Kidd. 31ᵃ יש מאכיל וכ׳ one may feed his father on pheasants &c.; a. fr.
- `8.1` Hithpa. a. Nithpa. to be consumed, burnt up, digested. Ber. VIII, 7 עד כדי שיִתְאַכֵּל וכ׳ Ar. (ed. שיתע׳) until the food is digested (or absorbed) in his bowels. Tam. II, 1; a. e.
- `8.2` Hithpa. a. Nithpa. to be worn off, spent. Snh. VI, 12 Y. ed. נתא׳ הבשר (Mish. ib. VI, 6 נתע׳) when the flesh of the corpse was gone. Cant. R. to IV, 4 שלא נתא׳ אחת מהן none of them was worn off. Kidd. 59ᵃ נתא׳ המעות the money was spent; a. e.

### `jastrow:אָמַר I` אָמַר I: (a) to join, knot; to be knotted, thick; b) to heap up; c) transf. to join words, compose, cmp. דבר; d) to contract, bargain, exchange. [As to Assyr. to see, cmp. חמא.])
- `1` (a) to join, knot; to be knotted, thick; b) to heap up; c) transf. to join words, compose, cmp. דבר; d) to contract, bargain, exchange. [As to Assyr. to see, cmp. חמא.])
- `2` to speak, think, say, relate &c. … א׳ ר׳ … א׳ ר׳ Rabbi … related in the name of R. … Ber. 3ᵇ; a. v. fr.—Part. pass. אָמוּר expression. Yoma 70ᵇ, a. fr. הוא הא׳ וכ׳ it is the same expression (‘one ram’) used here and in the Book of Numbers. Sifré Deut. s. 334, …
- `3` to vow, devote. אָמוּר (לגבוה) devoted to the Lord. Succ. 55ᵇ; v. אֲמִירָה I, 3, a. אֵימוּרִים.
- `4.1` Nif. (b. h.) to be said, to read … נא׳ כאן … ונ׳ להלן we read here … and we read there … (the same expression is used here and there in the Scriptural text). Ber. 9ᵃ; a. v. fr.—שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (abbr. שנ׳, שנא׳) for it is said in the Scriptures (as evidence in favor of …

### `jastrow:אָמַר II` אָמַר II: , v. אָמַר I, a) to be thick, strong.
- `1` , v. אָמַר I, a) to be thick, strong.
- `2.1` Hif. to thrive; to boast, vaunt, be oppressive (cmp. Ps. XCIV, 4). Sot. IX, 15 (49ᵇ) יוקר יַאֲמִיר the nobility shall be oppressive (Snh. 97ᵃ יְעַוֵּת; Der. Er. Zut. X יעוות; Cant. R. to II, 13 הווה).—*[
- `2.2` Hif. (denom. of מריא to fatten. Sabb. XXIV, 3 (155ᵇ) Bab. מַאֲמִירִין, Ms. Oxf., Mish. a. Y. מַמְרִים, מַמְרִין, v. מרי.]

### `jastrow:בָּטֵל I` בָּטֵל I: v. בטי) [to be hollow];
- `1` v. בטי) [to be hollow];
- `2` to be void, abolished, suspended; to cease to exist. Keth. 103ᵇ בָּטְלָה קדושה sanctity of life ceased; [oth. opin.: the levitical law concerning the contact with a corpse was suspended in favor of Rabbi; v. Tosaf. a. l.]. Ab. V, 16 ב׳ דבר בְּטֵלָה וכ׳ as soon…
- `3` to rest from labor, be at ease, be idle. Ab. IV, 10 אם בָּטַלְתָּ מן וכ׳ if thou choosest not to study the law, there will be many disturbances (excuses) to assist thee. Ib. I, 5 בּוֹטֵל מד"ת he neglects the study of the Law; a. fr.
- `4.1` Nif. to be abolished, suspended. Y. Meg. I, 70ᵈ bot. עתידין לִיבָּטֵל (= לְהִיבָּטֵל) shall in future be abolished (neglected). Gitt. 32ᵃ, v. infra.
- `4.2` Nif. to be excused, be exempt. Ib. II, 16 thou art not a free man להִבָּטֵל ממנה so as to be exempt from life’s duties.
- `4.3` Nif. to remain single. Gitt. IV, 5 (41ᵇ) יִבָּטֵל shall he never marry?
- `5.1` Pi. to abolish, suspend, cancel, undo, neglect. Ab. II, 4 בַּטֵּל רצונך וכ׳ set aside thy will for the sake of the Lord’s will, in order that He may set aside the will of others (euphem. for His will) for the sake of thy will (withdraw evil decrees at thy prayer).…
- `5.2` Pi. to neutralize an admixture of forbidden food &c. in a certain quantity. Ḥull. 108ᵃ ושאינו מינו … ומבטלו and the portion of the mixture which is not its kind is prevailing and neutralizes the forbidden portion (as if did not exist at all); a. fr.
- `5.3` Pi. to disturb, cause suspense, interfere with. Erub. 63ᵇ; Meg. 3ᵃ בִּיטַּלְתֶּם וכ׳ ye interfered with the daily offering; a. fr.—Ber. II, 5 לבטל ממני וכ׳ to suspend (shake off) the yoke of heavenly government even one minute.
- `6.1` Hif. to cause interruption, to order suspension. Succ. V, 5 להַבְטִיל את העם וכ׳ to cause the people to cease working.
- `7.1` Hithpa. a. Nithpa. to be interrupted &c., v. Nif.—Tan. d’be El. I, 5; II, 3.

### `jastrow:בָּטֵל II` בָּטֵל II (m.): (preced. ws.)
- `1` (preced. ws.)
- `2` null, void; vain. Gitt. IV, 1 ב׳ הוא is void (revoked); v. בְּטֵל Ithpe.; a. fr.—Pl. בְּטֵלִים, בְּטֵלִין, בְּטֵי׳.—דברים ב׳ vanities, absurdities. Ab. Zar. 16ᵇ; a. fr.
- `3` idle, unemployed, not needed. Snh. 21ᵃ one horse והוא ב׳ when not needed (for war). Koh. R. to VI, 8.—Pl. as supra. Y. Meg. I, 70ᵇ bot. עשרה בטילין … לביה"כ (where there are) ten men at leisure for attending Synagogue, v. בַּטְלָן. Ber. 16ᵃ והוא שב׳ ממלאכתן pr…

- `3` behold(h. . Dan. III, 25.—Targ. Gen. III, 22. Ib. XX, 15; a. fr.—Dan. II, 43 הֵא־כְדִי as if.—Targ. Ps. CXXIII, 2 הָא כמא as, … הא כדין Ms. (ed. הֵכְדֵין) so
- `4` introducing a question, is it that? Targ. Job XV, 7; a. e.—Ib. XXXVIII, 19 הא דין Ms. (ed. אֵידֵין) where?

### `jastrow:הָפַךְ` הָפַךְ: (b. h.)
- `1` (b. h.)
- `2` (act. verb) to turn; to change; to reverse; to pervert, subvert, destroy. Ex. R. s. 18 הָפְכָה המדינה שעבודה וכ׳ a country perverted the rules of forced labor for the captives (treating them inhumanely); ה׳ עליהם את הדין וכ׳ he changed the law against them and…
- `3` (neut. verb) to change. Sifra ch. IV אי כולי ה׳ לבן if the whole of it has turned white. Neg. IV, 3; a. fr.
- `4.1` Nif. to be upturned, destroyed; to be changed. Yalk. Esth. 1056 כשנ׳ סדום when Sodom was subverted. Gen. R. s. 50 ומדינה נֶהְפֶּכֶת, v. אִדְרַבְלָא. Tanḥ. Sh’moth 25 נ׳ להם מן וכ׳ the voice turned around, as if coming from &c.; a. fr.
- `4.2` Nif. to roll about, v. Hithpa.
- `5.1` Pi. to reverse, pervert, turn. Gen. R. s. 20 שהי׳ דברים וכ׳ who spoke perversely of the Creator. Kidd. 59ᵃ עני מְהַפֵּךְ וכ׳ a poor man turns the cake, and another comes and takes it, i.e. one who buys away what another is negotiating for.
- `5.2` Pi. to scheme, v. infra.
- `6.1` Hithpa. , Nithpa. נִתְהַפֵּךְ
- `6.2` Hithpa. to be changed, disguise one’s self. Gen. R. s. 21 end שהם מִתְהַפְּכִים they (the angels) assume various shapes.
- `6.3` Hithpa. to turn one’s self around. Ib. שהיא מִתְהַפֶּכֶת וכ׳ for it (the fiery sword) turns around man &c. Yeb. 35ᵃ; Keth. 37ᵃ מתהפכת she turns herself (makes violent motions). Ib. נִתְהַפְּכָה (Keth. l. c. נֶהֶפְכָה).—Tanḥ. Vayetsé 11 היה מ׳ עליו prevaricated (changi…
- `6.4` Hithpa. to scheme. Ruth R. introd. 3 שהוא מִתְהפֵּךְ ובא וכ׳ he (Esaw = Rome) schemes and comes against &c.; Yalk. Prov. 959 בא ומְהַפֵּךְ על וכ׳. Ib. שהם מִתְהַפְּכִים וכ׳; Ruth R. l. c. שמְהַפְּכִין.

### `jastrow:טוֹב I` טוֹב I: (b. h.) to be good, fit, handsome, valuable.
- `1` (b. h.) to be good, fit, handsome, valuable.
- `2.1` Hif. to prepare, outfit, dress, adorn. Gen. R. s. 26, v. טַבָּת. Gen. R. s. 83 (play on מהיטבאל, Gen. XXXVI, 39) מְטִיבֵי אלוהות היו they were dressers of idols; שהיו מֵטִיבִין עצמן וכ׳ they adorned themselves in honor of the idols. Ib. מטיבי נשים וכ׳ they dressed …
- `2.2` Hif. to turn a dream unto good (saying, ‘I have dreamt a good dream’). Ber. 55ᵇ יְטִיבֶנּוּ בפני וכ׳ he shall turn it in the presence of three persons, and say &c.
- `2.3` Hif. to do good, be beneficent. Ib. IX, 2 הטוב והמטיב who is good and beneficent. Taan. 31ᵃ תקנו … הטוב והמ׳ they introduced in Jabneh the benediction ‘who is good &c.’ (in the grace after meal).—Kidd. I, 10 מטיבין לו good will be done to him; a. fr.—Part. Hof. מוּ…
- `3.1` Pi. to improve a field, to till oftener than usual. Y. Shebi. IV, 35ᵇ top טִיְּיבָהּ וכ׳ if, after he improved a field, he died &c.
- `4.1` Nithpa. to be improved. Shebi. IV, 2; Tosef. ib. III, 10; a. e.—Denom. טִיּיוּב.

### `jastrow:טוֹב II` טוֹב II (f.): (b. h.)
- `1` (b. h.)
- `2` good; a good thing. Ber. 5ᵃ (ref. to Ps. XXXIX, 3) אין ט׳ אלא וכ׳ under good the Torah is meant. Ib. 60ᵇ זו מדה ט׳ this refers to a good dispensation; a. v. fr.—יום טוב (abbr. י"ט) festival. Bets. I, 1; a. fr.—כי טוב (ref. to Gen. I, 4) day-time. Pes. 2ᵃ.—Ber.…
- `3` noble, elder.—Pl. טוֹבִים. Tosef. Shek. II, 16 בני ט׳ of noble descent; a. fr.—טוֹבֵי העיר representatives of the town. Meg. 27ᵃ

### `jastrow:יְהוּדָה` יְהוּדָה: (b. h.) pr. n. Judah,
- `1` (b. h.) pr. n. Judah,
- `2` son of Jacob; tribe of Judah. Pes. 50ᵃ. Yoma 12ᵃ, a. e. מחלקו של י׳ from the area of Judah.—Gen. R. s. 85; a. v. fr.
- `3` name of several Tannaim; a) R. J. b. B’thera in Babylonia Ber. 22ᵃ; a. fr.—b) J. b. Tabbai, chief of the Sanhedrin in the days of queen Salome. Ab. I, 8. Ḥag. II, 2; a. fr.—c) R. J. the priest. Eduy. VIII, 3.—d) R. J. b. Baba. Ib. 2. Sabb. 62ᵇ; a. fr.—e) R. J.…
- `4` name of several Amoraim, the most renowned of whom is R. J. (b. Ezekiel), a Babylonian. Keth. 110ᵇ bot.—Y. Taan. I, 64ᵇ top; a. fr.—V. Fr. M’bo p. 91ᵃ
- `5` Judaea, the southern province of Palestine. Keth. I, 5 (12ᵃ); Tosef. ib. I, 4. Kidd. 6ᵃ; a. v. fr.

- `1` f. (rarely m.) (b. h.;
- `2` hand; forefoot; handle. Ex. R. s. 42, end מיד ליד from hand to hand, directly, opp. ע"י שלוחו through his messenger.—י׳ רחבה a wide hand, liberality. Y. Ḥag. I, 76ᶜ top עני ויָדוֹ ר׳ a poor man who is liberal, opp. י׳ מעוטה stingy.—Ḥull. 58ᵇ, a. fr. בְּיָד on …

### `jastrow:נָתַן` נָתַן: (b. h.) to give; to place, put. Gitt. I, 6 תֵּן גט וכ׳ give this letter of divorce to my wife. Ib. תְּנוּ גט וכ׳ give ye &c. Ib. לא יִתְּנוּ לאחר מיתה they must not deliver it after the man’s death; a. v. fr.—נשא ונ׳, v. נָשָׂא.—נ׳ עיניו ל (to put an eye upon)…
- `1` (b. h.) to give; to place, put. Gitt. I, 6 תֵּן גט וכ׳ give this letter of divorce to my wife. Ib. תְּנוּ גט וכ׳ give ye &c. Ib. לא יִתְּנוּ לאחר מיתה they must not deliver it after the man’s death; a. v. fr.—נשא ונ׳, v. נָשָׂא.—נ׳ עיניו ל (to put an eye upon)…
- `2.1` Nif. to be given, put &c.; to be intended. Ned. 38ᵃ לא נִיתְּנָה תורה אלא וכ׳ the Law was given only to Moses and his descendants. Ib. וכולן נִתְּנוּ וכ׳ and all those (gates of understanding) were granted to Moses; a. fr.—Keth. 81ᵃ לא ניתנה כתובה וכ׳ the K’thubah …
- `3.1` Hof. to be put. Makhsh. I, 1 בכי יוּתַּן (הרי זה) it comes under the law (Lev. XI, 38), i.e. it is a liquid which, if put on eatables, makes them susceptible of uncleanness. Ib. 2 אינן בכי יותן do not qualify for uncleanness; a. fr.

### `jastrow:עָבַר` עָבַר: (b. h.)
- `1` (b. h.)
- `2` to be thick, swell; to run over. Par. VI, 4 כדי שׁיַעַבְרוּ המים וכ׳ that the water may overflow into a vessel; a. e.—Ex. R. s. 15 שאני עוֹבֵר, v. עֶבְרָה.
- `3` (cmp. סְגֵי I a. II) to pass, to cross. Tosef. Sot. VIII, 1; Sot. 33ᵇ כיצד עָבְרוּ ישראל את וכ׳ in what manner did the Israelites cross the Jordan? Cant. R. to V, 13 עוֹבֵר הוא וחוזר וכ׳ he goes over his studies once and again &c.; a. fr.—ע׳ את הדין to cross t…

- `5` to transgress, sin. Yoma 86ᵇ, a. e. כיון שע׳ אדם עבירה ושנה וכ׳ as soon as a man has committed a sin and repeated it, it becomes to him a permitted act. Ib. 86ᵃ, a. e. ע׳ על עשה when one has transgressed a positive law (committed a sin of omission). Ib. ע׳ על …
- `6` to pass, overtake, precede. Pes. 7ᵇ כל המצות מברך עליהן עובר לעשייתן in the performance of all religious ceremonies one must say the benediction over them before doing them; מאי משמע דהאי עובר לישנא דאקדומי היא what evidence is there that this ‘ober has the me…
- `7` to pass by, go away, be removed. Pes. III, 1 אלו עוֹבְרִין בפסח the following things must pass away (their use must be suspended) during Passover. Bets. 26ᵃ, a. fr. מום עובר a transitory blemish, opp. קבוע. Ber. 26ᵃ ע׳ יומו בטל קרבנו when its day is passed, th…
- `8.1` Hif. to lead past, stroke gently. Tosef. Sabb. VII (VIII), 23 [read:] מַעֲבִירִין על גבי העין בשבת you are permitted to stroke a sore eye on the Sabbath; Y. ib. XIV, 14ᶜ bot. מעבירין על העין. Ib. הֶעֱבִירוּ עליו כלים they passed garments over it.
- `8.2` Hif. to cause to pass; to remove, displace. Keth. 17ᵃ מַעֲבִירִין את המת מלפני כלה וכ׳ a funeral procession is made to make way for a bridal procession, and both of them for the king, v. supra. Gitt. 57ᵇ שאין אנו מעבירין אותו באל אחר that we shall never displace hi…
- `8.3` Hif. to go beyond. Ib. איני מעביר וכ׳, v. supra. Ib. הבריות מעבירין על הדין וכ׳ men go beyond the line of justice (are treacherous, cruel), and they are punished; a. e.
- `8.4` Hif. to skip over; to forego, postpone.—ה׳ על מדותיו, v. מִדָּה.—Yoma 33ᵃ; 58ᵇ, a. fr. אין מעבירין על המצות you must not forego the occasion for performing a religious act. Erub. 64ᵇ אין מעבירין על האוכלין you must not pass by eatables (and let them lie in the stre…
- `8.5` Hif. to forego, overlook, pardon. R. Hash. 17ᵃ (ref. to Mic. VII, 18) מעביר ראשון ראשין וכך היא המדה (Ms. M. על ראשון וכ׳; v. Rabb. D. S. a. l. note) he pardons one sin after the other (before they are put on the scale), and such is the divine dealing. Ib. מעבירין …
- `8.6` Hif. to cause to forego or disregard. Erub. 41ᵇ שלשה דברים מעבירין … על וכ׳ three things make a man disregard his own sense (of right) and the sense of his Maker; a. e.
- `9.1` Pi. to be completed, full. Tem. 22ᵃ, a. e., v. supra.
- `9.2` Pi. to transgress repeatedly. Y. Keth. VII, 31ᶜ top, v. supra.
- `9.3` Pi. to go beyond. Ex. R. l. c. עיברה את הדין, v. supra. [Tosef. B. Kam. VI, 22 שעיברה הדליקה עד, read with Y. ib. VII, 5ᶜ. שעברה … את.]
